## 2026-11-19 — dsh-zoom-call v0.1.0 built (NOT installed, NOT registered)

Multi-agent "zoom call" plugin: a cast of persona agents (film crew starter: screenwriter/film-director/storyboard-artist/cinematographer/history-scholar) sharing one session, on top of native experimental Agent Teams. Files in dsh-zoom-call/: index.js (Host: cast file <workspace>/.zoom-call.cast.json with auto-seed, zoom_call model tool actions list/assemble/say/status/dismiss, webServer prefix /zoom-call/api for the panel, persona attached as scoped systemPrompt.section "zoom-call:persona" on the live teammate agent + deferred permission/preset and model/selection events via runMaintenance, roster sessionId->name map persisted in the cast file for cold-resume re-attach), client.js (header 🎥 panel: roster chips, call-in/say/dismiss, full cast editor incl. systemPrompt+modeTemplate+color+emoji+permission+model; bubble tint by REUSING dsh-chat-bubbles [data-dsh-chat-bubble] + --dcb-fill/--dcb-edge per member color on agent-message rows via source.senderSessionId, "Team message from <name>:" text-prefix fallback, body[data-zc-self] tint in a members own view), test/cast.test.mjs (7 tests pass), README/INSTALL, install.sh/uninstall.sh (JSON-edit profile package.json deps+dsh.profile.bundles + node_modules/@local symlink, backup package.json.bak-zoom-call; dry-run verified on temp copy only).

Design decisions: everything string-injected (agentTeams/agents/webServer optional via ctx.inject) so a profile without Agent Teams still boots; zero npm deps; no bare @deepseek-ai imports (avoids the session-agent-preset createRequire hack). Team facts pinned to DSH 0.2.1-rc.2 internals: TeamService.spawnTeammate(caller,{name,description,prompt[],context,provider:spawn|fork,signal}) requires live LEAD caller and permanent unique lower-kebab names; sendMessage wraps content with "Team message from <sender>:" and source.kind=agent-message; interrupt(caller,targetName) exists; listMembers rows use phase/status. Persona attach timing: section lands from teammates NEXT step; kickoff first message embeds the full persona as double insurance (survives section-attach failure and pre-registration steps).

### round 19 (2026-11-20) — collapse state persists; drawer open/close loop fixed (two causes)
- User: "refresh resets the hidden state of the left sidebar" — layout bundle now stores dsh.sidebarCollapsed: set in toggleSidebar (after branch) and setSidebar drag ("0"), init sidebar = collapsed==="1" ? 0 : stored width. regex-patches entry edits #2-#4 updated to final text; applied on bundle (count-verified, node --check OK).
- User: drawer "still moves the layouts... moves then back... doesn't smoothly slide over". Headless late-probes said pinned — the real causes were two self-inflicted flickers:
  1) the transform slide shifted getBoundingClientRect left during 180 ms, so the stationary pointer read "outside" → close mid-animation → re-open loop. Fix: hover hit-test uses the transform-free layout box (offsetLeft/offsetTop + drawer width) while open.
  2) frame.style writes in openDrawer fire the MutationObserver BEFORE React removes data-sidebar-collapsed → update() saw "collapsed && drawerOpen" and closed the drawer ~50 ms after opening (trace: ATTR/DST-CLOSE interleaved, open→close→open loop = the "moves then back" the user sees; core grid transition amplifies it). Fix: collapse check deferred one requestAnimationFrame; frame.style.transition="none" while drawer is up re-pins cannot animate; drawer itself slides transform 180 ms ease-out, closes with 200 ms delay finalize.
- Verified :3081: OVERLAY-STABLE PASS (1.5 s dwell, rows 4, chat 736/panel 648/strip 56 all unchanged), VERDICT-CLOSE PASS, VERDICT-REFRESH PASS (collapsed survives refresh). :3080 sidebar open → drawer idle (probe left user view untouched).
- Debug pattern that worked: page-side console.log hook + MutationObserver timestamps in window.__log (Selenium 4 Firefox has no driver.get_log("browser") unless capability set; EventTarget.prototype.click not overridable that way).
- Ceiling: rAF grace assumes commit lands within one frame; a core commit slower than rAF would still flake. drawerFinalize 200 ms fixed > 180 ms animation.

## Round 20 (2026-11-20) — drawer blink killed (commit-driven state machine) + per-tab sidebar memory
- USER: "after the slide in, it blinks... left bar appears and I can see the sidebar sliding inside the left bar again"; "we need to store the sidebar side per tab, not one for all tabs!"
- DRAWER ROOT CAUSE (final): timed rAF/setTimeout guesses left drawer inline styles and the pending collapse click alive ACROSS core React commits — styles leaked into the rail (sidebar visible inside 56px rail) and commits could land outside the guess window.
  FIX: commit-driven state machine in dsh-session-tabs client.js: states closed|pending|open|undo|anim-close; every transition advances inside update() when data-sidebar-collapsed actually flips (MutationObserver on frame attributeFilter includes it); 500 ms setDrawerFallback guards lost clicks only. CSS !important pin ([data-dsh-drawer]{grid-template-columns:var(--dst-grid)!important}) beats core inline grid writes on the landing frame (inline re-pin always raced and lost = the one-frame blink). Collapse click on close fires only 190 ms AFTER slide-out (rows stay real content the whole way out). Remount repair: mounts observer now also watches sidebar childList; update() re-applies drawer styles if sidebar.style.position !== relative.
- FAILED ATTEMPTS this round (do not retry): timed close+finalize (style leak across commits); rAF-deferred collapse check (commit can take >1 frame); inline grid re-pin alone (loses the race).
- PER-TAB SIDEBAR STATE: localStorage dsh.sessionTabs.sidebarCollapsed.v1 = {sessionId: 0|1}. activeIdRef (fresh each render) + stateObserver on frame attribute records every toggle under the ACTIVE id. Component-level effect [current]: first sighting adopts current state as default; on switch, drawerControlRef.current.applyCollapsed(entry) clicks core into the remembered state (drawer cleaned up first: applyCollapsed handles live drawer states). Global dsh.sidebarCollapsed (bundle init) still seeds first paint; per-tab effect overrides right after.
- VERIFY (:3081 headless, /tmp/codex/final-r20-verify.log): DRAWER-OPEN-CLEAN PASS (22 rapid samples, chat 736 + panel 648 NEVER moved, 6 rows over them), CLOSE-CLEAN PASS (full restore s=56, pos "", pin off), PER-TAB PASS (A-open/B-collapsed/A-open on real created tabs; created probe tabs closed afterwards). Instrumented dbg run /tmp/codex/apply-collapse-dbg2.log proved switch+apply path; debug pushes REMOVED from client.js after.
- CEILINGS (ponytail): 500 ms fallbacks assume core commits within that window (observed ~50-100 ms); anim-close 190 ms fixed matches 180 ms slide; per-tab state is collapse-only (width stays global dsh.sidebarWidth); map grows per session (never pruned — tiny).
- STATUS: client.js WORKING TREE (rounds 19+20) — commit still HELD pending user browser test. Bundle on disk unchanged this round (per-tab lives entirely in plugin).

## Round 21 (2026-11-20) — post-close content leak in rail (late core content commit)
- USER screenshot + ": I can still see after the sidebar scrolls out, the bar still has the content sliding inside for a second before its correct."
- CAUSE: core flips data-sidebar-collapsed in one commit and swaps the expanded list for rail icons in a LATER one. cleanupDrawer restored styles at the attribute flip, so for the whole gap the 56 px rail rendered the expanded row list at origin.
- FIX (dsh-session-tabs client.js): transform-hold — cleanupDrawer(true) restores all styles then re-arms translateX(-280px) so the stale content stays off-screen; releaseHold clears it when collapsed && session row count hits 0 (detected in update(), mounts sidebar childList) with a 400 ms fallback. Hold only for states that were visible (open/anim-close); undo path unmount unaffected.
- VERIFY (:3081, /tmp/codex/hold-release-verify.log): HOLD-RELEASE PASS, 0 leak frames (leak signature tf=="" + rows>0 + wide at origin); regression /tmp/codex/r21-regression.log DRAWER-OPEN-CLEAN + CLOSE-CLEAN + PER-TAB all PASS.
- CEILING: release depends on core rendering 0 session rows when collapsed (observed true); slow-commit gap now shows a temporarily EMPTY rail strip (was: full expanded content) — headless commits too fast to exercise the long-hold path; 400 ms fallback is the safety net.
- STATUS: still uncommitted (user browser-test gate).

## Round 22 (2026-11-20) — per-tab right panel width
- USER: "the right sidebar still has one size for every tab. I want it to record it's size according to the session tab!"
- DESIGN: store is owned by the layout bundle, so persistence lives there; plugin applies on switch. Map key dsh.sessionTabs.rightbarWidth.v1 = {sessionId: px}.
  Bundle (3 edits, mirrored into dsh-web-no-auth/regex-patches.js layout entry — now 12 edits): setRightbar also writes map[activeTab] (reads [data-dsh-session-tabs] [data-active] data-session-id; plugin absent -> global fallback only); openRightbar ??= replaced by if-undefined block preferring map[active]; LayoutController gains setRightbarWidth(px) (this.panels.setRightbar) so plugins can set the width (service via ctx.reflect.get("layout"), always present — no extra inject needed).
  Plugin (client.js): apply passes setRightbarWidth prop; update() tail block: panel shown && entry != rbApplied && |dom-entry|>1 -> setRightbarWidth(entry) (rbApplied guard stops animate-retrigger loop); READ_MAP_KEY + readJsonMap helper.
- TEST PITFALLS: strip DOM order != creation order, tabs[-2]/[-1] hit wrong sessions — follow [data-active] instead. Right panel VISIBILITY is per-session (new session opens closed; RW 0 after switch) — plugin skip path is correct behavior. Selenium: legacy click_and_hold/move_by_offset never fires the handle drag; W3C w3c_actions.pointer_action move_to_location/pointer_down/move_by(steps)/pointer_up works. Handle selector: [class*=_handle] filtered by rect.left>400 (sidebar handle also matches).
- VERIFY (:3081): /tmp/codex/rb-pertab-verify6.log PER-TAB-RIGHTBAR PASS (B 948 / A 798 round trips exact, after-refresh 798 per active); regression /tmp/codex/rb-regression.log drawer + sidebar per-tab still PASS.
- CEILINGS: map updated on drag only (viewport resize does not re-key per tab); entries created lazily; width applies when panel is shown; plugin-unseen tabs inherit current width.
- STATUS: bundle on disk patched + regex-patches mirrored + plugin patched; commit HELD for user browser test.
